## Slice 103: two panes on a Samsung Galaxy Z Fold's inner screen, live fold/unfold, keyboard shortcuts (after 0.10.6; no version bump, no artifacts)

All figures are from emulators set to published screen sizes (`wm size`, `wm density`) and invented data. **Nothing was run on a real Samsung Fold, tablet or iPad.**

**Root cause (the owner's report: no two panes when unfolded).** The old rule (`rememberIsWide`) asked for a window **840 dp wide**. A book-style foldable opened is never that wide at Samsung's default display size (420 dpi, 2.625): Z Fold 3/4/5 inner 1812 x 2176 px = **690 x 829 dp**, Fold 6 1856 x 2160 = **707 x 823**, Fold 7 1968 x 2184 = **750 x 832** (upright, and the same sizes turned: the long side is at most 832). Only a smaller display-size setting (373 dpi: 777 x 933) reaches 840, and then only on its side. Cover screens: Fold 5 904 x 2316 = 344 x 882, Fold 6 369 x 905, Fold 7 411 x 960, Flip 6 inner 411 x 1005 (phones: correct). The manifest had no `configChanges`, so every fold, unfold, rotation and window resize destroyed and rebuilt the activity (saved state brought back only the `rememberSaveable` parts). Before screenshots: `/tmp/fold/before/shots_before/fold5up_2open_entry.png` and `fold5land_2open_entry.png` (the entry covers the screen, no list beside it).

**The rule** (`core/domain/WindowLayout.kt`, one pure function, used by Android and iOS): two panes when the WINDOW is at least **600 dp wide and 480 dp high** (Material "Medium" width, not "Compact" height). Listed in `WindowLayoutTest` (11 tests): phones upright and on their side (800 x 360, 892 x 412, 956 x 440, SE 667 x 375: one pane); Fold covers and Flip (one pane); Fold 3-7 inner in both postures, Pixel Folds (two); Tab S9 800 x 1280 and 1280 x 800, iPad mini 744 x 1133 (two); split-screen halves (a Fold's half 414 x 690: one; a Tab landscape half 640 x 800: two; a third 427 x 800: one); DeX windows (1000 x 700 two, 500 x 700 one, 1000 x 450 one); the 600/599 and 480/479 edges. At the largest Android display size an upright Fold 5 is 531 dp wide: one pane, on purpose. List pane: 42% of the width, at least 340 dp (never more than half), at most 480.

**The fix.** (1) `WideFrame` keeps the screen in the same place of the composition in every mode (only modifiers change; Compose's `movableContentOf` was tried first and crashed with a focused text field, "Cannot get View because the Modifier node is not currently attached"), so a fold does not reset Home's scroll position; the open entry's typed draft is carried to the editor that appears in its new place (`restored`/`onDraft` in `EntryFormScreen`). (2) Manifest: `resizeableActivity="true"` and `configChanges="screenSize|smallestScreenSize|screenLayout|orientation|keyboard|keyboardHidden|navigation|density|uiMode"`; locale, layoutDirection and fontScale deliberately stay rebuilds. Dark mode switched live with the app open matches a fresh start (screenshot compared). (3) The height test is made on the window, which the keyboard does not shrink (device test: keyboard up on an 829 x ~600 dp window stays two panes). (4) **A bug found on the way, also on tablets before: an entry opened from Search, the calendar or any other screen was invisible on a wide window** (the editor was only drawn beside Home); it is now a full screen over that screen and Back returns to it (`anEntryOpenedFromSearch...` test). (5) Hinge (`androidx.window` 1.5.0 was already on the classpath through Compose and Glance; declared at the same version, APK +16 KB): a fold that separates the window and runs top to bottom puts Home and the entry either side of it; in table-top the app fills the half above the fold. Only started on a phone with a hinge-angle sensor. Tested only through a fake fold (`WideLayoutTestHook.hinge`); the window-info flow itself was never fed by a real or emulated fold. (6) Ctrl+N (Command) adds an expense and Ctrl+F searches, through `Activity.onKeyShortcut` (works whatever Compose has focused), only on Home, Search and Reports, listed in Meta + /. (7) `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` is new (measurements and verdicts).

**Screenshots read** (`/tmp/fold/after2/shots_after2/`, `/tmp/fold/font2/shots_font2/`, not in the repo): `fold5up_2open_entry` (690 x 829: list 340 + entry 350), `fold5land_2open_entry` and `fold5land_2b_keyboard` (829 x ~690, keyboard up, still two panes, Save above the keys), `tabs9_2open_entry` (800 x 1280), `foldcover_1home` (344 x 882 phone), `phoneland_2open_entry` (851 x 393: phone), `fold5up_6reports`, `fold5up_8review`, `fold5land_5search_open` (single pages, full width), `font2/fold5up_2open_entry` and `1b_scrolled` (font scale 2.0: readable, bottom bar labels tight in the 340 dp list as on a 360 dp phone at 2.0; chips and Save scroll). The Fold 7 (both ways up) and Flip shots were opened in the second pass (below).

**Tests.** Host: domain 670, parsers 184, storage 447 = 1,301, 0 failures (`--rerun-tasks`; +11 `WindowLayoutTest`). Native release guard (forced): domain 663, parsers 171, 0 failures. `:shared:compileKotlinIosSimulatorArm64` compiles; `tools/ios_build.sh unit` 39 of 39. Device, API 34 (cold-booted, `tools/emulator_setup.sh`): WidePaneUiTest 12 + KeyboardShortcutsUiTest 3 + HomeLayoutUiTest, HomeListTest, SearchUiTest, AccessibilityUiTest, UxPolishUiTest = 50 tests, 0 failures; then widget classes (WidgetDesign, WidgetEyeTap, QuickAddWidget, ForegroundRefresh, WidgetTargets), Capture, CaptureRules, Inbox, InboxSwipe, IncomeCapture, SearchBulkCategory, SearchDates, SearchStart, SavedSearch, BulkEdit, Categorise, Categories, Accounts, AccountAsk, AccountPeriod, AccountFilter, AccountTiles, TransferCandidates, Motion, Appearance, EditDelete = 222 tests, 0 failures. `tools/release_smoke.sh` API 34: 14 of 14; `tools/check_release_keeps.py`: PASS (9,436 classes). Local `bundlePlaySms`: 35,216,943 bytes against 35,182,606 for 0.10.6 (+34 KB).

**Final verification (a second pass, on 84ea840 and the commits before it).**
- **Full API 26 device suite** (110 classes, the four timing classes excluded, cold-booted with `tools/emulator_setup.sh`, cold-booted again halfway, batches of 6 classes): first half 55 classes, **380 tests, 0 failures**; second half 55 classes, 344 tests, **4 failures, all `WidePaneUiTest`** (the tests that change the display with `wm size`), run on 67c3540. Root causes, found by reproducing on the emulator: (1) **a real bug on Android 8: `LocalWindowInfo.containerSize` did not follow a live resize** (the window got wider, the layout stayed one pane; a window that opens already wide was right). Fixed: on Android the size now comes from the configuration (`screenWidthDp`, `screenHeightDp`; `WindowSize.android.kt`, `expect/actual`, iOS keeps the window info). It would also have hit a DeX window dragged wider on an Android 9 or 10 phone. (2) Two tests failed to launch ("main thread has not gone idle within 45 s") because they set the display and launched in the same second, which Android 8 handles slowly: the test helper now waits 1.5 s after every `wm` command (a race of the test, not of the app). After both: `WidePaneUiTest` 12 of 12, twice, on API 26; then 12 more classes that touch this code (KeyboardShortcuts, HomeLayout, HomeList, HomeFilterScroll, TransferCandidates, Motion, Search, Accessibility, UxPolish, MoreMenu, Onboarding, Guide) **100 tests, 0 failures**. The rest of the suite was not run again after the last two small commits (the configuration source and the test wait): only those classes were.
- **Release smoke:** API 26 (release build, R8) **7 passed, 7 widget steps skipped (Android 8)**; API 34 again on the final code **14 of 14**; `tools/check_release_keeps.py` PASS (9,436 classes).
- **API 34 on the final code:** 262 tests in 31 classes (WidePane, KeyboardShortcuts, HomeLayout, HomeList, Search, Accessibility, UxPolish, the five widget classes, capture, inbox, search, bulk, categorise and account classes, Motion), **0 failures**.
- **Timing gate A/B against 06c540e** (API 26, cold boot before each run, order old new new old old new, host load 4.2 to 6.6): all six runs 15 of 15; the 34 `PERF` medians differ by 0.92 to 1.14 times; no regression (details in `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`).
- **Size and start on the final build:** `bundlePlaySms` built locally 35,219,660 bytes against 35,182,606 for 0.10.6 (+0.11%); cold start `am start -W` median 281 ms against 289 ms for 0.10.6 (24 alternating starts each, load 8 to 14); idle PSS 61.0 MB against 61.0 MB.
- **Screenshots opened now:** Fold 7 inner upright (1968 x 2184 px at 420 dpi = 750 x 832 dp: list and entry side by side, nothing clipped), Fold 7 on its side (832 x 750: the same), Flip inner (1080 x 2640 px = 411 x 1005 dp: the phone layout, the Home screen whole). Plus, on API 26, a live unfold from 344 dp to 690 dp after the fix.
- **Host and native:** unchanged since the first pass (the domain code did not change); the iOS compile passes after the `expect/actual` was added. **`tools/ios_build.sh unit` could NOT be run again on the final commit:** `xcrun simctl list` hung on this Mac (CoreSimulator wedged; killing its service did not help), so the build never started. The 39 of 39 is from the commit before the `expect/actual` (iOS code path: `platformWindowSizeDp()` returns null there, so iOS still uses the window info as before).

**Performance decisions** (see `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`): cold start `am start -W` base 322 ms, final 304 ms (alternating, n = 24 each, host load 12-18); one change reverted (lazy WorkManager: process start -7 ms, TotalTime not better); `Application.onCreate` costs under 1 ms; idle PSS within +0.1 to +0.3 MB of base (noise range); the 700 ms "data composed" on this emulator is its software drawing, not app code.

## Slice 104: the type on To review cards (Expense / Income / Transfer) and card bill payment texts (the owner's two tester reports; after 0.10.6; no version bump, no artifacts)

All texts, names, digits and amounts in tests and here are invented, in the shapes of the owner's reports. **Nothing was run on a real phone or on real bank traffic.**

### A. "While reviewing there is no option to choose Transfer. Only Expense or Income."
- **Root cause.** The To review card chose its type only from the text: money out was an expense, money in was income, and a transfer appeared only when the words said a cash withdrawal or a card payment (`KindRules`), or when two waiting texts paired. A person who knew the text was a move between their own accounts, and whose digits the app could not pair, had no button for it.
- **Design.** Every ordinary card has three chips, `inbox_kind_<id>_EXPENSE|INCOME|TRANSFER`, starting as the text says. Expense and Income turn a text read the wrong way round (the old flip). Transfer asks only the other account: "To account" for money out, "From account" for money in; the text's own account is the one it names or the existing "Which account is this?" (Slice 99). The other account is suggested from what the person did before, the account the text names by its digits, the only Cash account for an ATM text, or the only other account. It saves as a real TRANSFER through `EntryDrafts` (same currency, no double count, reference and text kept as origin), with "Added as a transfer to X · Undo" (`CaptureAdds`, `ADDED_UNDO`). An ATM text starts as a cash withdrawal only with exactly ONE Cash account (before: the first one of several).
- **Learning** (`TransferLearning`, `transfer_rule:` settings, device-local, `accountId|uses|against`): keys per payee and direction, and per wording (bank, direction, a closed list of transfer words, the other account's digits); the rails IMPS/NEFT/RTGS/UPI never make a key. One transfer starts the next text as a transfer to check; Add all and a swipe take it only after THREE transfers to the same account with no exception (`SuggestionConfidence` reused, bar raised from two to three on purpose); an expense of that kind counts against the rule.
- **Add all and swipe.** Add all takes only cards that need no decision (clear expenses/income and strong learned transfers); its dialog says how many are transfers ("N are transfers between your own accounts (₹X): not spending or income"). A swipe approves only a card still in the type it started as.
- **The other leg.** After a text is added as a transfer, the text for its other side is recognised as the same transfer (amount exactly, the text's account is the transfer's other end, within 30 minutes, a day when it names the other account's digits, or the same reference): "Add to that transfer" (`CaptureAdds.join`: the text joins the entry's words, one entry), "Skip", "It is a different payment". A text pointing the same way as a text the entry already holds is a repeat, not the other leg. The pair-of-references rule of `Duplicates.best` is not applied here (as for two waiting texts, `TransferPairs`), because it hid the other leg of an own-account move whose two texts carry different references.
- **Strings:** "To account", "From account", "Add as transfer", "Add to that transfer", "It is a different payment", "Transfer into/out of X", "Suggested: ...", "You made texts like this a transfer N times before, so it starts as one. Check it.", "Added as a transfer to X", "Added to the transfer".

### B. A card bill payment confirmation was shown as a NEW BILL
Text shape: `Hi <NAME>, Axis Bank Credit Card bill payment of Rs. 12,345.67 was successful! T&C -Kotak811` (invented numbers).
- **Root cause (which stage).** Not the sender and not the bill matcher: the CLASSIFIER's bill rule. `GenericAlertParser` read it as a debit, but `CaptureClassifier` then asked `BillParser`, whose cue `bill ... of` matched "bill payment of", whose receipt guard (`completed`) did not know "was successful" and could not cross the dot of "Rs." (`[^.]{0,40}`), and `MoneyMoved` found no past-tense movement word, so it became `CaptureOutcome.Bill` (a credit card bill of Rs 12,345.67 titled with the SENDER's bank). The sender: the app allowlists `KOTAKB` only; `AX-KOTAK811-S` is NOT allowlisted (no public trace of KOTAK811, KMBL or KOTAKM as an Indian bank header was found; `KOTAKB` is on public sender directories), so the owner's text was captured through `KOTAKB` or through the iPhone paste/Shortcuts path (no sender: the text names Axis Bank, which was taken as the sender; now the other named bank, Kotak, is chosen for a bill payment, and `BankRule.textAliases` lets "Kotak811" say Kotak). If the owner's real header differs, "Senders we skipped" lists it and "Treat NAME as my bank" adds it (see "Sender question" below).
- **Fix, parser** (`BillPayments`, `CaptureClassifier`, `BillParser`): payment confirmations are read BEFORE the bill rule, as a transaction (PAID: money out of the paying account; RECEIVED: money in on the card or at the biller), never a bill and never plain spending. `BillParser.completed` now knows "payment ... was successful" across the dot. A failed or declined payment that asks for nothing is ignored (it was a "bill" too). The generic alert is kept when its direction agrees (every corpus field unchanged); for the paying side the account is only the one the words name (never the card's digits). Invented corpus `tools/sms-corpus/invented/bill_payments.tsv` (`BillPaymentCorpusTest`): payments found 46/46, false friends kept 44/44, confirmations read as a bill 0, bills read as a payment 0; `BillPaymentsTest` 42 positive variants with exact side, kind, amount, card bank, digits, biller; 20+ false friends (bill generated or due, statements, promos, OTP, failed, pending, scheduled, autopay, plain debits and credits, refunds, card spends). One existing expectation moved: "Thank you for your payment of Rs.5,000.00 towards your HDFC Bank Credit Card XX0862" was ignored, now a CREDIT (the requested shape).
- **UX** (`BillPaymentRows`, `BillPaymentMatch`, `BillPaymentBox`): the card says **Bill payment** and asks "Mark the Axis card bill as paid?" (the card's bank) or "Mark this bill as paid?", lists the open bill it pays (found or tracked, or a card statement worked out from billing dates; same bank or the digits of the card the words name or the card account they resolve to; the total, the minimum, or anything between for a card; a rupee or 1% for any other bill; the due date from 45 days before to 30 after), with **Mark paid**, **Not this** (an ordinary card) and **Ignore**. Mark paid records the payment the way the Bills page's Mark paid does (a transfer from the paying account to the card account, else an expense in Bills), dated from the text, the text as origin, and marks the bill paid with that date; "Marked X as paid · Undo" (`BillStore.unpayTo`: the bill back exactly as it was). With no open bill the card says "No open bill matches this payment" and offers "Record a card bill payment of ₹X". The card's own later text of the same payment (or the bank's after the card's) is recognised ("Already in your ledger" / "You already marked X as paid"), button "Same payment: add to it" (one entry, Undo), also when the first payment was an expense in Bills (the card's credit text never becomes income).
- **Old wrong bills: opt-in, never on open** (safety review of the first version, which moved them automatically in `MessageIngest.reclassifyWaiting`; that automatic move was REMOVED because it rewrote the person's existing data without being asked, and `reclassifyWaiting` now only keeps its old pending-to-bill direction). Now the top of the Bills page shows a card (`bills_repair_card`): "N bills below are really confirmations of payments ("bill payment ... was successful"), made by an earlier version. Nothing is due. Move them to To review, where each can mark the bill it paid." with the button **"Move to To review"** (`bills_repair_move`), only for a bill that passes every one of these (`BillPaymentRepair.notice`, `MessageIngest.repairableBills`, the latter built only while the Bills page is open, so Home and the save path never run it): it was FOUND from an SMS (never a tracked bill the person added, never an email or a manual one); it is still FOUND (not paid, not edited into TRACKED, not skipped); it has no entry and no paid date; its stored words are a payment confirmation (`BillPayments.read`), do NOT parse as a bill (`BillParser.parse` null) and do not ask for money (`BillParser.asksForMoney`: no deadline, no minimum due, no total due); the bill amount equals the payment amount; and the bill's due date is empty or equals the text's date. Anything else (a real bill, an edited bill, a paid bill, a bill with a different due date, a bill from another source) is left exactly as it is and never offered. A bill has no attachments (those belong to entries), so the guard on "attached" data is the entry link and the paid date. The move is ONE transaction (`BillRepairs.move`: adds the text to To review and skips the bill, and fails if the bill is no longer FOUND); the skipped bill row is kept as DISMISSED, never deleted, and the text is kept. "Moved N bill(s) to To review · Undo" (`BillRepairs.undo`: the bill back as FOUND with its words, and the untouched To review item the move made is taken away again so the move can be done again; an item the person has handled since is left exactly as they left it). A real bug the device run found here: the first Undo left the skipped item behind with its fingerprint, so a second move had nothing to add; fixed and tested (`BillRepairsTest`, `BillPaymentUiTest`).
- **0.10.6 databases.** `SchemaFrom0106Test` builds the schema of e7dea55 (the commit before this work; `schema-0.10.6.sql`, 53 statements) in a fresh SQLite file with old rows (a FOUND payment bill, a TRACKED bill, an inbox item), runs `ensureLatest` twice and asserts: the current shape equals the 0.10.6 shape (no table or column changed; four queries were added to `Schema.sq`: `entriesNearBillPayment`, `transferLegsNear`, `unpayBillTo`, `deletePendingInbox`), nothing changes on the second run, old rows survive, the new queries run, and the move and its undo work on that old database. Host tests for "edited or paid bills are left alone": `BillPaymentRepairTest` (5: a real bill or a text that also asks for money is never moved; a bill the person acted on is left alone; a due date that is not the text's own is left alone; only a text bill with its words and the same amount qualifies; the owner's text is repairable) and `BillRepairsTest` (6: the text waits in To review with its original words and the bill is skipped, not deleted; a bill acted on since changes nothing and leaves no item behind; it cannot run twice and the same text already in To review is not made twice; Undo puts the bill back with its words, takes away the untouched item the move made and the move can be done again; an item the person has handled since is left as they left it; no ledger entry is touched and it costs nothing on a 20,000-entry ledger).
- **Existing tests adapted, with the reason.** `PairingUiTest` (2 tests): the card's own "payment received" and "credited to your ... Card" texts are now Bill payment cards (the spec's own variants), so they are recorded or joined with the new buttons; the ledger result is the same (one transfer, never income). `PairingUiTest` and `OwnTransferUiTest`: the list is scrolled to find a card (a card is taller now). A cash withdrawal with two Cash accounts no longer starts as a transfer to the first.
- **iPhone.** The same parser and `MessageIngest` run; `CaptureTests.testACardBillPaymentConfirmationIsAPaymentAndNeverANewBill` was added (with a header, pasted with none, and a generated bill staying a bill). `tools/ios_build.sh unit` ran on the final tree (CoreSimulator was not wedged, no restart needed): 40 unit tests including this one, 0 failures; the shared framework compiled for the simulator.

### Sender question for the owner: where did "-Kotak811" come from, and what should he check on his phone
- **What the code does (checked, not guessed).** `BankRules` has one Kotak header root, `KOTAKB`. A search of public sender-ID directories and Kotak's own pages (which are JavaScript-rendered and could not be read) found no trace of `KOTAK811`, `KMBL` or `KOTAKM` as an Indian bank header, so none was added. "Kotak811" is the SIGNATURE at the end of the text ("T&C -Kotak811"), not necessarily the header it arrived from. `BankRule.textAliases` ("kotak mahindra", "kotak811", "kotak") now lets a text without a sender (iPhone paste or a Shortcuts automation that passes none) say Kotak from its words, and for a bill payment the bank other than the card's bank is chosen (so the Axis card in the words no longer becomes the sender).
- **If his real header is not `KOTAKB`** (for example `AX-KOTAK811-S`): the text is skipped before any word is read. The existing "Senders we skipped" list (More, Capture; shown for headers ending -S or -T) then shows `KOTAK811` with a count and "Treat KOTAK811 as my bank" (also typed under "Your bank's sender name"); only texts that arrive after that are read, because a skipped text is never kept. `BillPaymentUiTest.aKotak811HeaderThatIsNotOnTheListCapturesNothingIsOfferedUnderCaptureAndTrustingItCapturesTheNextPaymentText` runs this flow on a device: `AX-KOTAK811-S` captured nothing, `skipped_sender_KOTAK811` was listed, `skipped_trust_KOTAK811` added `sender_chip_KOTAK811`, and the next payment text from that header was captured and shown as a Bill payment card.
- **What he must look at, on his phone** (nothing here can): in his messages app, open the conversation of that payment text and read the NAME shown at the top exactly (`AX-KOTAK811-S`, `VM-KOTAKB-S`, `KOTAKB`, a phone number...). A name ending -P or -G, or a phone number, is not offered by the list (write it down for the next release). On the iPhone the Shortcuts automation must pass the "Sender" with the text; without it the words decide.

### Tests and runs (final tree, one change since: a test-only wait in `IncomeCaptureUiTest`)
- **Host (JVM, `--rerun-tasks`):** domain 686, parsers 199, storage 463 = 1,348 tests, 0 failures, 0 skipped (new in this work: `TransferLearningTest` 6, `BillPaymentMatchTest` 10, `CaptureAddsTest` 8, `BillPaymentsTest`, `BillPaymentCorpusTest`, `BillPaymentRepairTest` 5, `BillRepairsTest` 6, `SchemaFrom0106Test` 2). Native release guard (optimised Kotlin/Native on the simulator): domain 679, parsers 185, 0 failures. iOS unit: 40, 0 failures.
- **API 34, cold boot + `tools/emulator_setup.sh`, 26 classes in 6 batches of at most 6 (the 15 requested plus `BillEntriesSaveTest`, `BillItemsUiTest`, `BillsRepeatingUiTest`, `CaptureRulesUiTest`, `IncomeCaptureUiTest`, `NotificationsTest`, `OriginUiTest`, `RulesUndoUiTest`, `SkippedSendersUiTest`, `SkipUndoUiTest`, `TrustedSendersUiTest`): 225 of 225 pass.** First runs failed 9 tests: 7 in `BillsUiTest` (the helper that settles a bill "from To review" waited for an Add button; a payment confirmation is now a Bill payment card and a plain debit card has `inbox_add_`; helper rewritten to handle both) and 2 in `BillPaymentUiTest` (a REAL bug: Undo of the repair left the skipped item with its fingerprint, so a second move found nothing to add; fixed in `BillRepairs.undo`, which now removes the untouched item the move made; and a test that did not leave the Bills page before opening To review).
- **API 26, cold boot, `tools/emulator_setup.sh`, the FULL suite: 112 classes (the 4 timing classes excluded) in 19 batches of at most 6 with a second cold boot before batch 10: 753 tests, 3 skipped by their own assumptions, 2 failed, 751 passed.** The 15 requested classes are inside it and all passed. The 2 failures: `IncomeCaptureUiTest.aCreditFromAPersonOffersIncomeChipsAndOneChoiceIsLearnedForThePayerAsIncomeOnly` (test-side race: it scrolled to the card the moment the screen opened, before the card was composed on the slow emulator; the helper now waits and scrolls again; 6 of 6 twice before and twice after the fix) and `DataTest.anEncryptedBackupAsksForThePassphraseOnTheScreenAndAWrongOneIsSaidOutLoud` (`waitFor("open_passphrase")` timed out once, after the emulator had been up for an hour; 14 of 14 on each of two reruns alone; the backup code and test were not touched by this work; the cause was NOT proven, a slow emulator is an inference).
- **Real bugs found by the device runs and fixed:** (1) the other leg was looked for only among entries `Duplicates.best` accepted, which rejects two different bank references, so the credit of an own-account move with its own reference was not recognised; now looked for among the transfer's own legs. (2) A bill payment card offered a bill of another card of the same bank; the digits of the card account the text resolves to now count. (3) Undo of "Move to To review" left a dismissed item whose fingerprint blocked a second move.
- **Existing tests adapted, with the reason:** `PairingUiTest` (2), `OwnTransferUiTest` and `PairingUiTest` (scroll to a taller card), `BillsUiTest` (settle helper; a payment confirmation is a Bill payment card), `InboxKindUiTest.theSamePayee...` (a learned transfer already starts the second text as a transfer), `IncomeCaptureUiTest.goes` (waits for the card).

One run of each build failed that one p95 limit (12.5 ms): new 13.2 ms (host load 6.0 at its start), base 20.1 ms (load 4.9); 12 of the 14 runs passed 15 of 15. Home and the save path are untouched (`LedgerStore`, `EntryActions` and the Home list are not in the diff; `repairableBills` runs only while the Bills page is open); `BillPayments.read` measured 36 µs a text on the JVM and `inboxRows` does one more read and two settings lookups a text.

The reference of the two legs is not compared when joining. `KOTAK811` was not added as a header (no evidence). The repair of old wrong bills is opt-in, narrow, one transaction and undoable (above), never on open.

### Release 0.11.0 (Android code 40, iPhone build 37): integration
Ships Slice 103 (fold and large-screen two panes, live resize, hinge, keyboard shortcuts, `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`) and Slice 104 (Expense / Income / Transfer on every To review card, learned transfers, bill-payment recognition "Mark the Axis card bill as paid?", the opt-in Bills repair card) on top of 0.10.6 (code 39, published to production by Google; 40 is the next code). The app keeps its name **Expenses**: a rename to "Rupee Log" was started and withdrawn by the owner before anything was pushed or released (it was removed from the tree by resetting the local, unpushed commit; nothing of it is in the repository except `docs/RENAME.md`, now a checklist "not applied", and `IdentifierGuardTest`). Every build and run below is of the tree WITHOUT the rename (the first runs done with it were discarded and redone).

**Review of 06c540e..HEAD (risk).** Fold layout: `resizeableActivity="true"` and `configChanges` for size, orientation, density, uiMode, keyboard and navigation (locale, layout direction and font scale still rebuild the activity, as before); the layout rule is one pure function (`WindowLayout`, 600 x 480 dp, host-tested), the hinge watcher is started only on a phone with `android.hardware.sensor.hinge_angle` (nothing is loaded elsewhere), Ctrl+N and Ctrl+F are tried after the focused view (a text field keeps its own keys) and do nothing where their screen would stay hidden. `ExpensesApp.onConfigurationChanged` still redraws the widgets on Android 11 and older. To review: transfer learning keeps device-local settings (`transfer_rule:` prefix, never in a backup), a transfer is `SURE` only after three consistent uses with no exception (a wrong transfer hides money from both spending and income, so the bar is higher than for a category), rails (IMPS, NEFT, RTGS, UPI) never make a key by themselves. Bill payments: `BillPayments.read` is judged before the bill rule, a failed payment is not a bill, the invented corpus has precision tests; the repair card moves a wrong bill to To review only on the person's tap, narrowly, with Undo (`BillRepairs`, all or nothing). Schema: additive only (`SchemaFrom0106Test` upgrades a real 0.10.6 schema and checks nothing is lost). **Nothing needed fixing in the product.** What the runs found were test faults (below). Not verified: a real foldable (the layout was driven by resizing emulator windows and the tests' display-size changes), a real hinge, a real hardware keyboard.

Both now grant it in `@Before`. (The logcat showed the listener bound and connected; nothing else was wrong.)
