## Slice 108: two names of one merchant, and suggestions that look at the amount (after 0.11.0; no version bump, no artifacts)

**All names, amounts and choices in tests and here are invented. Nothing was run on a real phone or on real bank traffic.**

### A. "Same shop as": merchant aliases
- **Built in (`MerchantAliases`, domain).** Seven groups of starter-list merchants that are ONE merchant and are filed the same way: Amazon Pay is Amazon; Prime Video is Amazon Prime; JioCinema, Disney+ Hotstar and Voot are JioHotstar; Paytm FASTag is FASTag; INOX is PVR; Vistara is Air India; IXIGO Trains is ixigo. `PayeeKey.of` applies it (the key of an alias is its canonical merchant's), so what is learned and what history says is pooled. `MerchantAliasesTest` refuses a group whose members differ in starter category or better home, so a merge can never change what the starter list suggests. Six legal or descriptor names were added to existing merchants (innovative retail concepts, delightful gourmet, fashnear technologies, oravel stays, amzn mktp). Merchants of one company that are filed differently stay apart on purpose (Swiggy / Swiggy Instamart, Amazon / Amazon Fresh, Jio / JioMart / Jio-bp, Uber / Uber Eats). **Precision on invented names: 51 false-friend pairs (Uber Singh, Jio Mart vs Jio, Apollo Tyres vs Apollo, ...) merged wrongly 0; every wording of the seven groups reaches one key.** The subscription finder does NOT use the built-in table (a subscription hidden under its old name must not come back).
- **The person's own (`PayeeAliases`, `CaptureRuleStore.addAlias/removeAlias/snapshotAlias/restoreAlias`).** A device-local setting `payee_alias:<key>` = `<key it counts as> TAB <name> TAB <other name>`: no schema change, no migration, no entry is ever rewritten. Chains are followed (limit 8), a cycle or a shop with itself is refused with a plain message. Where it applies: (1) what is learned: a choice for one name is found for the other, and learning through either writes ONE row for the shop (the name's own older row is kept, so removing the alias gives it back); (2) history and amount bands of both names count together (extra indexed lookups only for a payee that has an alias); (3) a search of one or two words that names such a shop also finds the other names (`AliasSearch`, newest first, filters kept); (4) the subscription finder counts the names as one merchant; (5) the Merchant report adds them into one row under the name the person gave and drilling in returns exactly its total (`ReportStore`); (6) the Categorise review and "File N earlier expenses" group by the shop.
- **Screens.** More, Capture, Category rules, below "What the app has learned": "Names counted as one shop (N)" with a row per alias and **Remove**, a button "Count two names as one shop", and on every learned payee **"Same shop as…"**; a learned name that counts as another says "Counts as Swiggy: what is learned for it is used for this name too." On a To review card: Change, then **"Same shop as…"** (the other waiting payees are offered as chips). Creating and removing an alias each have an Undo bar ("Swiggy Instamart now counts as Swiggy", "Swiggy Instamart is its own shop again"; on the card the To review bar, on the rules page its own bar); Undo restores what the name counted as before exactly, and is refused with a message when it would now make a cycle. Reports page note: "Names you counted as one shop (More, Capture, Category rules) are added together."
- **Backups (added after the first push).** Aliases travel in the backup's `settings` map as `payee_alias:<key>` entries, the way card limits (`card_limit:`) and rates (`rate_`) do, which is how this format already carries per-key device settings (learned categories are the `merchantMap` section; their list labels are not in a backup). Export adds the keys; a restore takes an alias only for a name the phone has no alias for, only when it makes no cycle and no self-alias with what is here, reading the file in key order (one file always gives one result), writes it back clean and takes at most 2,000 (`BackupStore.MAX_ALIASES`). Additive and backward compatible: a backup without the keys restores exactly as before; an older app restores only the settings it knows by name or prefix, so it ignores them (checked in code: `k in SETTINGS || card_limit: || rate_`); `tools/backup_interop/Interop.java` and the iPhone tests treat `settings` as an opaque map and need no change. `docs/BACKUP_FORMAT.md` has the paragraph; tests are `PayeeAliasBackupTest` (5).

### B. Amount-aware suggestions (`AmountBands`, `SuggestionSource.AMOUNT`)
- **No new schema.** The evidence is the person's own filed entries for the payee, which already carry amount and category: the same indexed lookup of the payee's words as the history suggestion (`ExpensesDb.payeeCandidates`: the 300 newest; if that is full and the name has several words, a second all-words lookup so a shared word like "shop" cannot crowd the payee's own rows out). `similarCandidates` now also returns the entry's currency (additive column of a SELECT). A category the person changes on an entry changes what is suggested.
- **Rule.** Nearest neighbours, not buckets: an earlier amount is "like" this one when the larger is within 125% of the smaller (₹199 is near ₹160 to ₹248). Only for a payee filed under at least two categories (a payee with one category is answered by the plain history, unchanged); at least 2 nearby entries in one category and strictly more than any other (a tie says nothing); same currency; payees matched by `PayeeKey` (spelling, built-in and own aliases), not by one name containing another. It comes right after the person's rules and before "what you chose last time". **CHECK** unless `SuggestionConfidence.AGREE_MIXED` (3) or more nearby entries agree and none of the nearby entries is in another category (then SURE, and Add all / a swipe may take it). Never applied by itself. Works for money out and for money in (`adviseIncome`, the editor's income suggestion).
- **What the card says.** The category line has no parenthesis for this source; a line `inbox_why_<id>` says: "You chose Subscriptions for Amazon amounts like ₹199 (4 times)"; with conflicts "(4 times; once something else)"; for money in "You chose Salary for money from Acme Ltd of amounts like ₹52,000 (3 times)". The note in the card list is "4 of your earlier entries for amounts like ₹199". Other categories of the payee are offered as "Or X" chips.
- **Invented simulation (`AmountBandsTest`, printed).** One mixed payee, 12 new amounts: banded 6, right 6, wrong 0, sure 5 (right 5); the other 6 (₹12, 450, 850, 1,200, 5,500, 60,000) fall back to the payee-level suggestion. Six invented mixed payees (Amazon, Paytm, Google Play, Apple, Flipkart, a corner shop), 300 new amounts each, categories separated by amount: with 120 earlier entries each the payee-level suggestion alone is right 42%, a band speaks for 99% and is right 99%, amount-aware overall 98%, sure 1,653 all right; 10% random earlier choices: 39% / 99% / 98%, sure 831 all right; 30 earlier entries: band speaks for 78%, right 98%, overall 85%; 10 earlier entries: speaks for 28%, right 94%, overall 54% (the rest falls back). **Overlapping amounts (a Rs 400 order is as likely a bill as a purchase): the band speaks for 98% and is right 75%, overall 74% against 50% for the payee level; the 223 "sure" ones were all right** (they only occur where 3+ agree and none disagrees). These are invented clusters, written by me: how real prices overlap is not measured.
- **Cost (20,000-entry ledger, host, `AmountBandScaleTest`, a busy shared machine).** A payee lookup (indexed rows + history profile + band) median 23.8 ms, p95 50 ms over 300 lookups; a rare payee's lookup returns its own 40 rows (plus the capped 300 for the common word it shares), a heavy one is capped at 300. Only in To review (`inboxRows`, once per waiting item, the same lookups as before; no extra lookup unless the name has several words and a full result, or an alias). Nothing on Home or the save path (`confirmCapture` is unchanged: it learns as before; an accepted amount suggestion teaches nothing, the entry itself becomes evidence).

### Tests (host)
New: `MerchantAliasesTest` 8, `PayeeAliasesTest` 11, `AmountBandsTest` 12 (incl. the simulations), `CaptureCategoryAmountTest` 7 (parsers), `PayeeAliasStoreTest` 9 (CRUD and Undo snapshots, refusals, learning through a joined name, built-in carry-over, Merchant report and drill, search, and **a differential test: with no alias, 14 searches with filters return exactly the ids the plain search returns and the Merchant report is exactly the rows of the old query, and an unrelated alias changes neither**), `PayeeAliasBackupTest` 5, `AmountBandScaleTest` 1. Existing tests adapted: none needed; `PayeeKeyTest`, `DefaultRulesTest`, `CaptureCategoryTest` (Amazon Pay learned under the old key `amazonpay` is still found), `GroupedReportTest`, `LearnedChoicesScaleTest`, `BackupStoreTest` pass. **Final host run (`--rerun-tasks`, after pulling the other workers' commits): domain 758, parsers 221, storage 544 = 1,523 tests, 0 failures** (the `ReplacedBackupTest` failure seen earlier was the other worker's work in progress and passes now). **Native release guard (forced): domain 751, parsers 207, 0 failures. iOS simulator compile of `:shared`: OK.**

### Device tests (`SameShopUiTest`, 5, invented shops and amounts)
Written and compiled: a name joined to another from the learned list with Undo, Remove with Undo (what was learned and the entries stay); a typed name in the dialog and the refusal of one shop with itself, shown on the screen; a name joined from a To review card (Change, "Same shop as…") with the To review Undo bar; the reason line "You chose Subscriptions for Amazon amounts like ₹199 (3 times; once something else)" with its check line, and "(3 times)" for ₹2,499 with no check line, nothing added by itself; the same card at twice the font size with the dialog's buttons reachable.

### An incident on the way (said plainly)
My first push (`e5246c7`) broke `:shared` on origin/main: I staged only some -U0 hunks of `LedgerViewModel.kt`, `AppRoot.kt` and `CaptureScreens.kt` with `git apply --unidiff-zero`, which places a hunk by its NEW-side line number, so after other hunks were left out mine landed in the wrong places (inside a doc comment; `payeeAliases`, `joinShops` and `removeAlias` were missing). Fixed in `82ee179` by a three-way merge in a clean worktree and verified there: `:shared:compileAndroidMain`, `:androidApp:compileDebugKotlin`, `:androidApp:compileDebugAndroidTestKotlin` and the iOS simulator compile all pass. `AliasSearch` also used the JVM-only `putIfAbsent` (iOS compile); fixed. Rule kept since: no partial hunks of shared files without compiling the staged result in a clean worktree.

### NOT done, said plainly
- **Device, API 34 (one batch of 6 classes ran; the rest did not):** `SameShopUiTest` 5 of 5 passed, `InboxUiTest` 14, `InboxKindUiTest` 12 and `InboxSwipeUiTest` 4 passed; `LearnedChoicesUiTest` 3 of 5 and `CaptureRulesUiTest` 3 of 4. **The failures were my change's doing, not flakes** (`LearnedChoicesUiTest.theListOfWhatWasLearnedShowsItAndForgetRemovesIt` and `...earlierExpensesWithNoCategoryAreFiledUnderWhatWasLearnedAndUndoPutsThemBack` waited for a learned row that the new "Names counted as one shop" block, placed above the rows, had pushed below the visible list; `CaptureRulesUiTest.theFirstRuleWinsAndMovingARuleUpChangesTheSuggestion` timed out in its rule helper, cause not isolated). Fix in `1fe08c7`: the block now comes AFTER the learned rows, so the rows keep their place; **that fix and the `CaptureRulesUiTest` failure were not re-run** (not rerun alone twice as asked). The next three batches (Categor*, Suggested*, Subscriptions*, Reports*, Accessibility, Search*) got no result: the emulator stopped answering `adb` (a 6-minute timeout) and I stopped looping on the shared emulator when asked to.
- **Not run:** API 26 at all; the remaining API 34 classes above; the `RefreshBudgetTest` A/B against 56696a3 and the `PerformanceBudgetTest` search A/B (no baseline build could be made: the disk had 0 to 2 GB free); `tools/ios_build.sh unit` for this slice (a first run was stopped when the disk ran out). The search change cannot slow a user without aliases: `searchFiltered(query, aliases)` returns the plain `searchFiltered(query)` result at once when `aliases.isEmpty` (tested: identical ids for 17 queries), and a one- or two-word query is only widened when it names a shop the person joined.
- Searching a name written with no word in common with its alias (e.g. "amzn" for Amazon) is not found by the amount evidence (the lookup is by the payee's words).

## Slice 109: a type change is recorded in backups, so a restore never brings the replaced entry back (after Slice 108; no version bump, no artifacts)

Fixes the documented, unfixed double count of Slice 93 P2-7 and Slice 92: a type change (or a pair conversion) soft-deletes the old entry and makes a new one; a backup carries live entries only, so restoring an OLDER backup into a ledger that had the newer state (or OLD then NEW on a fresh phone) brought the replaced original back live next to its replacement. Entries with a bank reference were caught by the reference check; entries typed by hand and the absorbed income of a pair (`<ref>/reversal`) were not.
- **Design.** A small additive table `entry_replaced(entry_id, replaced_by, reason)` (`ReplacedEntries`, created by `Schema.sq` and by `SchemaUpgrade.ensureLatest`; the schema version is not raised, as for every table since the first release). Written by `KindChanges.change` (CHANGED: the editor, bulk "Make transfer", Transfer candidates for the expense/income itself), `KindChanges.absorb` (PAIRED: the income of a pair; `EntryActions.makeTransfers` calls it) and `CaptureTransfers.replaceEarlier` (MATCHED: a text in To review made an earlier entry a transfer). Dropped by `LedgerStore.restore`, the one way a deleted entry comes back to life, so Undo of any of the three removes it. Each record and removal is an `op_log` row (`entry_replaced`, `replaced_by`). Records chain (A, B, C) and stay when the replacement is deleted. Exported as the optional payload list `replaced` (`BackupReplaced`: `entryId`, `replacedBy`, `reason`; left out when empty, so a ledger without a type change writes the byte-identical old file; version stays 1 or 2).
- **Restore (`ReplacementPlan`, one plan for the preview and the restore).** (a) an incoming entry that is not here and has a record (here or in the file) is not added, whether or not its replacement still exists (deleted B keeps A out); a refund of it goes with it; (b) an entry live here that the file says was replaced is deleted (ordinary soft delete) when something later in its chain is live here or arrives in the file, its bank reference goes to the arriving replacement and its attachments move to it; never when nothing live follows (a chain ending in a deletion), never an entry with refunds, "I expect this back", a bill it pays or a repeating item that posted it (it stays, and the replacement is not added next to it); (c) records of the file are kept except for an entry that stays live; a record the phone already has wins, and a file's different replacement of the same original (a branch) is not added; (d) the duplicate-reference guard is unchanged; plain deletes behave exactly as before. A file record about an entry the same file lists as live, with equal ids or empty ids, and loops are ignored. Report: `RestoreReport.replacedNotRestored` / `replacedRemoved`; the dialog and the message say it in plain words (`restore_replaced`, `restore_removed`; "Nothing is overwritten or deleted." only when nothing is taken away).
- **Compatibility.** Older apps (0.11.0 and before, Android and the iPhone's earlier builds) ignore the unknown key `replaced` and restore the rest as before (their double-count risk remains for the replaced entries); a backup without the key restores exactly as before (`BackupErasRegressionTest`: three payloads in the 0.9.0, 0.10.4 and 0.11.0 shapes, plus a differential test of 200 random old-format files against the old rule). `tools/backup_interop/Interop.java` ignores unknown keys, now also validates `replaced` (`replaced=N`, `make-replaced`); the iPhone shares the Kotlin code.
- **Limits.** A backup made between a change and its Undo, restored later, applies the change again; an edit made to an entry here that is then taken away is not carried to its replacement; the file date is not compared with anything. In all of these the amount is counted once, never twice.
- **Tests run (JVM, private copy of the tree, `:core:storage:jvmTest`): 538 tests, 0 failures**, among them `ReplacedBackupTest` (23: every restore order of a three-link chain with and without a reference on a fresh phone and on the phone that made it, deleted replacement, Undo and redo, pair, refunds, built-on entries, files moved, sealed EXPENC2/EXPENC3 and plain files, the Java reference, 20,000 entries + 2,200 records: 6.4 s without records, 7.0 s with 2,000 of them taking away a live entry, export of 2,000 records 2 ms, a random model of 60 lives and 720 restore sequences), `KindChangeBackupTest` (the pinned double-count tests now pin the fix), `KindChangesTest` +3, `CaptureTransfersTest` +1, `SchemaFrom0106Test`, `SchemaUpgradeTest`, `BackupErasRegressionTest` (6), `BackupFormatDocTest`.
- **Final verification on a clean worktree of origin/main (b1c83db, then the fix 5d0fcb6), forced reruns:** host JVM `--rerun-tasks`: core:domain 734, core:parsers 206, core:storage 542 tests, 0 failures; native release guard (optimised Kotlin/Native binary, `--rerun-tasks`): domain 727, parsers 192, 0 failures. The `:shared` iOS compile of b1c83db failed, and the cause was found: partial `git apply --unidiff-zero` hunks of two workers landed in the wrong data classes of `LedgerViewModel.kt` (mine: `replacedNotRestored`/`replacedRemoved` in `DataUi` instead of `PendingRestoreUi`, fixed in 5d0fcb6; the others' were fixed by their owner); after that LearningMore reported the clean origin/main compiling for `:shared:compileKotlinIosSimulatorArm64`, `:androidApp:compileDebugKotlin` and `:androidApp:compileDebugAndroidTestKotlin`. `java tools/backup_interop/Interop.java verify2 - iosApp/Tests/Fixtures/reference-replaced-new.expbak replaced=1` passes.
- **NOT run (blocker: the disk, 0.7 to 2.4 GB free for hours, and the emulator lock held by other runs for over two hours):** device classes on API 34 and API 26 (`ReplacedBackupUiTest`, `BackupFilesTest`, `DataTest`, `ChangeTypeUiTest`, `TransferCandidatesUiTest`, `BulkEditTest`, `HistoryUiTest`, `AttachmentsTest`, `FixBalanceBackupDeviceTest`; they and `ScratchReplacedShots` compile, and the test APK built at 11:30 contains them), `tools/ios_build.sh unit` (with the Swift test `testAReplacedEntryIsNotBroughtBackInEitherOrderOnIPhone`) and `interop`, the screenshots of `restore_replaced` / `restore_removed` at 393 dp and 360 dp with font 2.0, and the hand-checked end-to-end restore in both orders on the emulator. `ScratchReplacedShots` (uncommitted, in the shared tree) does that end-to-end run with balance assertions (no spending, net worth 10,000.00) and the four screenshots per order when someone has the disk and the lock. One thing to look at in those screenshots: the dialog's text column in `DataScreen.kt` does not scroll, so at font 2.0 the new paragraphs may be cut off (a `verticalScroll` on that column is the fix if so).

## Slice 107: "Why isn't capture working?", the guided setup and "Bring in earlier spending" (after 0.11.0; no version bump)

New users get stuck at setup, so three related features. All names, digits and texts in tests are invented.

- **Checkup** (More, Capture, first button; also More, an empty To review, Home's Getting started card): `CaptureCheckup.rows` (pure, `core/domain`) turns the phone's state into rows with OK / Fix this / Note in words, a one-tap action each, and the top line "Everything looks fine" / "N things need attention". Checks: text permission and switch, notification access and listener connected (re-read every 2 s while open), reminders permission (Android 13+), battery (Unrestricted / Optimised / Restricted), a tick line for what no app can see (Samsung "Settings > Battery > Background usage limits > Deep sleeping apps", other makers' lists), each bank (account names and the setup's picks) "texts from HDFCBK or HDFCBN recognised" / "No text from ICICI Bank seen yet", Senders we skipped with "Treat NAME as my bank", accounts with a bank's name and no 3 or 4 digits, payments found in 7 days and skip reasons over 30. iPhone: a three-line Shortcuts tick list until a message reached the app in 7 days. Reads: a few settings, the capture log's grouped counts, accounts and phone status; never a message.
- **Check a message**: paste (or Android Share to Expenses) one text and an optional sender name; `MessageCheck` (pure, `core/parsers`) calls the same `PasteRules.classify` as real intake (moved from `MessageIngest.kt`, which now calls it) and shows sender status, bank, direction, amount, payee, matching account, suggested category, or why it would be ignored; a sender on no list also shows what it would be if trusted. Nothing is saved, counted or sent; only the last two account digits are shown; the words live in memory and go when the page closes. The Play SMS declaration is unchanged: nothing reads the inbox.
- **Guided setup** (`CaptureSetup`, `BankChoices`, `ui/GuidedSetup.kt`): four skippable steps (permission or Shortcuts, banks as chips from `BankRules` plus "My bank is not listed" using the Capture screen's sender box, accounts with digits saved by the Accounts page's own call, summary and checkup). State in one setting `capture_setup`. Offered after the first tour: opened at once when the tour is gone through to its last page, otherwise only a Home card (`SETUP` nudge, priority 6, before Getting started; "Not now" a day, "Hide" for good). `GuideUiTest` was adapted for this.
- **Bring in earlier spending** (More; Getting started link): three ways in recommended order (the existing statement importer, a fast form starting on last month's end that keeps date, category and account, Accounts for opening balances and Fix this balance) and a plain note that old texts cannot be read.
- **Tests.** Host: `CaptureCheckupTest` 15 (every combination, 13,824 input sets), `CaptureSetupTest` 9, `MessageCheckTest` 10, `BankChoicesTest` 5, `HomeNudgesTest` 9, `UiConventionsTest` extended: all passed when run alone. Device classes written: `CaptureCheckupUiTest`, `GuidedSetupUiTest`, `EarlierSpendingUiTest`, `ZzCheckupResumeUiTest`, a scan in `AccessibilityUiTest`. `CheckupTestHook` stands in for the phone's answers.
- **Verification that ran (clean worktree of origin/main c5610da, which contains 6d4d4c1):** host JVM `--rerun-tasks`: domain 758, parsers 221, storage 543 (incl. `UiConventionsTest`) = 1,522 tests, 0 failures; native release guard (forced): domain 751, parsers 207, 0 failures; `:shared:compileKotlinIosSimulatorArm64` OK; `:androidApp:assembleDebug` and `assembleDebugAndroidTest` built.
- **Did NOT run (state it plainly):** every device class (`CaptureCheckupUiTest`, `GuidedSetupUiTest`, `EarlierSpendingUiTest`, `ZzCheckupResumeUiTest`, the checkup scan in `AccessibilityUiTest`, the adapted `GuideUiTest`) on API 34 and API 26. The shared emulator lock was held back to back by another run for over an hour, and when my turn came the emulator refused to start ("not enough disk space to run avd API34", 2.0 GB free). `tools/ios_build.sh unit` started and died on "No space left on device" (not a test failure). Screenshots (393/360 dp, light/dark, font 2.0) were not taken, so no layout has been looked at: clipped text at font 2.0 is untested. `assembleRelease` and `tools/check_release_keeps.py` were not run after the new `SEND` filter.
- **The `ACTION_SEND text/plain` filter (checked by reading):** the only manifest change is that one `<intent-filter>` on `MainActivity` (already exported); the merged debug manifest still lists exactly the nine permissions `PermissionNotes` describes (no INTERNET, nothing added). Nothing else in the Android app receives `ACTION_SEND` (the one `ACTION_SEND` in `MainActivity` is the outgoing backup share). The shared words go only to `Check a message` (`takeShared` drops the extra from the intent and keeps it in memory; `check` reads accounts, rules and categories and writes nothing; `capture_event`, the inbox, bills and the skipped counters are asserted unchanged by `CaptureCheckupUiTest`, not yet run). PRIVACY_POLICY and PLAY_SMS_DECLARATION stay true, so they are unchanged.
- **For the integrator, device classes of this slice:** `CaptureCheckupUiTest` (11), `GuidedSetupUiTest` (8), `EarlierSpendingUiTest` (6), `ZzCheckupResumeUiTest` (2, grants RECEIVE_SMS: run last), `GuideUiTest` (changed), `AccessibilityUiTest` (new `captureCheckupSetupAndEarlierSpending`), then Capture*/Inbox*/Home*/MoreMenu*/PermissionResume*/TrustedSenders*/SkippedSenders*/WidePaneUiTest/UxPolishUiTest/FirstWeekUiTest. Also run `assembleRelease` and `tools/check_release_keeps.py`, and look at the screens at 360/393 dp, dark, font 2.0.

## Slice 105: Fix this balance (the owner's credit card report; after 0.11.0; no version bump, no artifacts)

All amounts and names in tests and here are invented. **The device tests were written and compile but were NOT run on an emulator (the shared emulator lock was held by other workers for hours and the disk fell below 1 GB); nothing was run on a real phone.**

**The report.** A credit card's balance is wrong when the app was installed after some purchases: the real bill is higher than the app knows, so after paying the real bill the card shows "extra balance". The owner fixed it by hand (difference between what the app shows and the bill, then edited the opening balance).

**Model chosen: (a) the difference moves the account's OPENING balance.** No new entry kind, no schema change. Why: the opening balance already exists and is read by every balance query (`accountBalances`, `accountBalanceBefore` that the card statement, summary, utilisation and due reminder use, `Ledger.balances` in memory, net worth), so every figure follows at once and the SQL and in-memory balances stay equal; no entry is made, so spending, income, categories, budgets, reports, subscriptions and Transfer candidates cannot change (tested: `spendByCategory`, the period report, the entry count, the candidate scan and the subscription samples are identical before and after). The change is logged like any edit of the account (`opening_minor` in the op log). **Effect on the past, said plainly:** every balance of the account at every date moves by the difference (the missing spending counts as owed "since before the first entry the app knows"), so a card's past statement figures move too; if the missing spending was only after the latest statement, "left to pay on the statement" shows too much. The balance-based safe to spend follows the corrected balances (that is its input); the budget figures do not move.

**Where.** More, Accounts, tap an account: the Edit account page has "Balance now" and **Fix this balance** (every kind of account, archived too). For a credit card that shows extra money, a hint line above the cards ("Looks like some earlier spending is missing." with the card's name, **Fix this balance**, **Dismiss**); Dismiss is a setting of the phone (`fix_hint_off:<id>`), one read per refresh for cards in credit only, no scan; answering a fix turns it off too.

**Strings.** Title "Fix this balance"; button "Fix balance"; "The app thinks you owe ₹3,000.00" / "...you owe nothing" / "...you owe nothing and have ₹2,000.00 extra in credit" (card, loan) and "The app thinks Savings has ₹25,000.00" / "...is overdrawn by ₹40.00" (bank, cash, wallet); the question by kind ("What does your bank app say?", "How much cash do you have right now?", "What does the wallet app say?", "What does your card statement or bank app say?"); one field "Amount you owe now" (card, loan) or "Balance in your bank app" / "Cash you have now" / "Balance in the wallet app" with two chips: "I owe" / "In credit (nothing owed)", "It has money" / "Overdrawn" (the card convention needs the chip because the app stores owed as negative and a card can really be in credit); live "Difference: ₹2,500.00 (the app is missing spending of ₹2,500.00)" / "(the app is missing payments or refunds of ...)" / "(the app is missing money that came in: ...)"; "Already matches: the app shows the same amount."; effect "After fixing, X will show ₹5,500.00 owed. No spending or income is added, so your budgets and reports stay as they are." (a card adds "The balance counts as owed since before your first entry here, so the statement and limit figures follow."); refusals "Type the amount to see the difference.", "This ledger is in ₹, so only ₹ amounts can be used here.", "Type the amount without a minus sign. Use the choice below for \"in credit\" or \"overdrawn\".", "Enter the amount as a number, like 25000 or 1250.50", "That amount is too large for the app to hold."; Undo bar "Balance fixed: ₹3,000.00 owed → ₹5,500.00 owed" then "Put the balance back."; history line "Balance fixed: ₹3,000.00 owed → ₹5,500.00 owed on 11 Nov 2026" (newest first, five shown). A typed "₹" or "Rs." is accepted. The Fix button is disabled until there is a real difference.

**Behaviour matrix.** Card owes more than the app: spending is missing, opening goes down. Card in credit after paying the real bill (the owner's case): type 0 owed, difference = the extra, balance 0, statement "paid". Card owes less: payments or refunds missing. Bank/cash/wallet: less in the bank than the app says = spending missing, more = money received missing; "Overdrawn" allowed, so the opening balance can go below zero (the Edit form now keeps an opening balance it shows as stored when its text is not changed, instead of refusing "-500.00"). Zero difference, empty, another currency, minus sign, nonsense, beyond 2^53 - 1 minor units: refused in words, nothing changes. Undo moves the opening balance back by the same difference (an edit made meanwhile is kept) and takes that fix's line away.

**Backup and compatibility (docs/BACKUP_FORMAT.md).** New optional payload key `balanceFixes` (`accountId`, `at`, `beforeMinor`, `afterMinor`), left out when empty, so a backup of a ledger that never used it is byte-for-byte what it was and an older reader ignores it; the opening balances are in `accounts` as always. Restored only for an account the restore adds. EXPENC1/2/3 only seal the same plain file (tested plain, EXPENC2, EXPENC3 and the plain version 1 file EXPENC1 wraps; EXPENC1 itself is in `FixBalanceBackupDeviceTest`, not run). **A bug found on the way and fixed narrowly:** a restore into a fresh app never took the opening balance of the default Cash and Bank accounts (they exist already, so the "already here" rule skipped them), so a fixed or set opening balance of the default Bank was lost; now an untouched first-start `cash`/`bank` (no entries on the phone, opening zero, same kind and currency, nothing refers to it) takes the backup's opening balance and lines (tested, including every case where it must not).

**Limits found.** The balance queries return a double, exact only to 2^53 - 1 minor units, so that is the limit of a fix (`BalanceFix.LIMIT`); saving an entry above about ₹3 crore fails in the spend-line allocation (pre-existing, not touched).

**Tests.** Host, all passing in a clean worktree of origin/main plus this slice: `BalanceFixTest` 16 (domain: signs of every kind, the owner's case, words, parsing, 2^53 limit, log), `BalanceFixesTest` 19 (storage: card/bank/cash cases, owner's case with statement and utilisation, due reminder, zero difference, currency, deleted account, huge amounts, Undo exact and as a difference, SQL = in-memory balances, totals and balances at earlier dates, Transfer candidates / subscription samples / card payment transfer untouched, history cap, hint flags, op log), `BalanceFixBackupTest` 10 (round trip, no key when none, older reader, merge rules, first-start rule, hostile lists, sealed layers); `BackupFormatDocTest` and `UiConventionsTest` pass. Device, written, compile, not run: `FixBalanceUiTest` 8 (dialog, live difference, fix, balance updates with no stale frame, Undo, refusals, chips, bank, cash, hint appears / opens / dismisses / persists, Hide amounts, dialog width) and `FixBalanceBackupDeviceTest` 4 (plain, EXPENC1, EXPENC2, no fixes + default Bank).

**NOT done / unverified.** The full host suites with `--rerun-tasks`, the native release guard, the iOS simulator compile and `tools/ios_build.sh unit`, the device classes on API 34 and API 26 (mine and the existing account, card, bill, backup, transfer, accessibility and wide-pane classes), screenshots at 393 and 360 dp in light and dark (a scratch test exists, not committed), real data and a real phone. The pushed code compiled for Android (shared, androidApp, androidTest) in a clean worktree of origin/main plus this slice.


## Slice 106: the Search page redesign: one box, one row of filters, one summary line, and a Filters panel (after 0.11.0; no version bump, no artifacts)

All names, merchants and amounts in tests and screenshots are invented. **Nothing was run on a real phone, an iPhone or with TalkBack.** Product code: `ui/SearchScreen.kt` (rewritten body), `ui/SearchFilters.kt` (new); `LedgerViewModel` / `SearchUi` / the query are untouched (the same search, the same debounce, no extra scan).

**What was wrong (read from the API 34 screenshots at 393 dp, `before_a393l_*`, light, invented data).** Everything a person could filter by sat above the results in one scrolling list: a saved-searches row, a wrapping block of 4 rows of chips (category, tag, Expenses, Income, then Any account / Bank / Cash / HDFC / ICICI), a row of day chips with From/To date buttons, and two amount boxes: **6 rows, about 60% of the screen** (`before_a393l_01`), results started at 960 of 1,568 px (`before_a393l_04`). Typing scrolled that block away (`before_a393l_02`), so no filter was visible while results were, and after choosing filters the page sat half-scrolled with chips cut through the middle (`before_a393l_06`). A chip that was ON differed from one that was off only by a faint grey tint (no tick, no cross on most), so what was on could not be read; the same filter could be on and unseen. The count, the spent total and Select all were an ordinary list item that scrolled away with the results. Saved-search chips were 40 dp high (under 48 dp); the keyboard covered the lower half whenever typing.

**The design.**
- **Top, never scrolls:** the search box (clear cross, `search_clear`) and a **Filters** button beside it with a badge of how many filters are on (`search_filters_button`, `search_filters_badge`; read as "Filters, 2 active"; from 150% text size it is the icon alone so the box keeps its room). Dates, account, category, tag, amounts and type each count once; the typed words do not.
- **ONE row of chips** (`search_active_chips`): every filter that is on is a filled chip with a cross that takes it off (`search_range_THIS_PERIOD`, `search_kind_expense`, `search_kind_income` for the three quick ones, `search_active_chip_range|dates|account|category|tag|amount` for the rest; read as "Remove filter: category Food"); the quick chips that are off follow, so a filter is never shown twice. Scrolls sideways with the fade, back to its start when a filter comes or goes.
- **ONE summary line** (`search_summary`; count `search_count` "23 results · ₹12,345 spent", Select all, "Searching…" `search_working`, the problem `search_problem`, the bulk change's notice and Undo under it): outside the list, so it stays put; a polite live region. Hidden when nothing matches (the empty page says it).
- **Filters panel** (`filters_panel`, scrim `filters_scrim`): over the page, not a window (the app has had lost taps in dialog windows with the keyboard); a sheet from the bottom (at most 90% high) on a phone, a card at the side of at most 480 dp (62% of a narrower window) when the window is two-pane wide. Sections: **Saved searches** (chips `filters_saved_<name>`; press and hold removes; **Save this search** `save_search` while there is something to save), **Dates** (`filters_range_THIS_PERIOD`, `search_range_LAST_30_DAYS`, `search_range_THIS_YEAR`, From/To `search_date_from|to` with `_clear`, `search_date_summary`, `search_date_problem`), **Type** (`filters_kind_any|expense|income`), **Account** (`search_account_any`, `search_account_<name>`), **Category** (`search_category`, `search_cat_*`), **Tag** (`search_tag`, `search_tag_*`), **Amount** (`search_min`, `search_max`, `filters_problem`). Chosen chips show a tick and the app colour. **Every choice searches at once** (so no second query exists for the "live count"): the button says "Show 23 results" (`filters_apply`, text `filters_count`; "Show results", "Searching…", "Show the 100 most recent"); Apply (or the cross `filters_close`, the dimmed page, Back) only closes, keeping the choices; **Reset** (`filters_reset`) puts the filters back as they were when it opened; **Clear all** (`filters_clear`) takes every filter off, keeping the words. Choosing a saved search closes it.
- **Start page** (nothing asked): the hint now points at Filters, then the saved searches (`saved_<name>`, one tap), recent searches, Try cards. **No results:** "No entries match", then one tap **Clear filters** (`search_none_clear`, or `search_none_clear_filters` when words are typed too, with "Clear the search" `search_none_clear` under it).
- **Keyboard:** unchanged rule (not opened by itself from an account or on coming back from an entry; Done and any chip dismiss it; a drag on the results dismisses it); the old "move the page up under the box" trick is gone because the filters are no longer in the list. Selection mode and the bulk bar are as before (opening it closes the panel). 48 dp: Back, Select all, Undo, Clear, the recent Clear, saved chips and every panel button.
- Four measurement marks (`search: tapped|composed|input|results`, logged only in measurement builds) and `tools/search_ab.py` (alternating A/B on the 100,000-entry dataset): see below.

**Tests.** New `SearchFiltersUiTest` (8): badge count and its description; an active chip removes itself and the count follows; the panel changes the results at once, the button's count, Reset, Clear all, Apply; save and use a saved search from the panel; opened from an account (no keyboard, the account is a chip); reading order and a summary that does not scroll; 200% font (everything displayed, button at least 48 dp, Apply inside the panel); a Fold-sized two-pane window (`wm size 1812x2176`, density 420: panel at most 480 dp, at the side, results behind). **Adapted, minimally** (the filters are now in the panel or chips): `SearchUiTest` (amounts, category and kind, the date chip), `SearchDatesUiTest` (opens the panel first; a saved search is applied from the panel and its days are then a chip), `SavedSearchUiTest` (`filters_saved_*` in the panel, `saved_*` on the start page), `TagsUiTest` (tag chosen in the panel), `AccountPeriodUiTest` and `UxPolishUiTest` (the account is a chip; the other accounts are in the panel). Untouched and expected to pass: `SearchStartUiTest`, `SearchBulkCategoryUiTest`, `BulkEditTest`, `ChangeTypeUiTest`, `WidePaneUiTest`, `RecreateStressTest`, `AccessibilityUiTest`, `KeyboardShortcutsUiTest`, `MotionUiTest`, `HomeFilterScrollTest`.

**What ran (API 34 emulator, debug build, cold-booted, the code of the redesign before the last cosmetic edits listed below).**
- Batch 1: `SearchUiTest`, `SearchDatesUiTest`, `SavedSearchUiTest`, `SearchStartUiTest`, `SearchBulkCategoryUiTest`, `SearchFiltersUiTest`: **35 tests, 0 failures** (`SearchFiltersUiTest` first run 6 of 8: the two failures were my test reading a chip's text where the chip has a spoken description; fixed in the test, then green).
- Batch 2: `AccountPeriodUiTest`, `TagsUiTest`, `BulkEditTest`, `WidePaneUiTest`, `UxPolishUiTest`, `ChangeTypeUiTest`: **57 tests, 5 failures**, all on Home (a Home row not found: `AccountPeriodUiTest` 1, `TagsUiTest` 1 (bulk tagging), `BulkEditTest` 3) on a just-booted emulator under heavy host load. At the parent commit (origin/main, no redesign) `AccountPeriodUiTest` + `TagsUiTest` + `BulkEditTest` passed 16 of 16; on the redesign, run twice alone: first run 1 failure (`TagsUiTest.bulkTagging...`, `ComposeNotIdleException: Idling resource timed out`), second run 16 of 16. Not reproduced twice, cause not established (the failing steps are Home's bulk bar, which this slice does not touch); the integration run decides.
- Screenshots (`~/Documents/personal/expenses_builds/search_shots/before|after`, not in the repo): the 393 dp light set was read and is clean (start page with saved searches, typed with the keyboard, quick chips, the panel, the panel with a keyboard, no results with "Clear filters", a filtered result with the one chip row, the selection bar). **The 393 dark, 360 light and dark, font 2.0 and Fold sets were taken while the emulator showed a "System UI isn't responding" dialog (host and disk under load), so they are dimmed and covered; they were read only for layout (they do show: the font 2.0 icon button and chips, the Fold side card).** They have to be taken again on a healthy emulator (`ScratchSearchShots.kt.txt` and `shots.sh.txt` are kept beside them; the scratch test is not in the repo).
- **Edits after those runs (not re-run on a device):** the badge sits further out of the corner (it covered the icon at font 2.0); the box says "Search entries" (at 360 dp "Merchant or note" was cut); the wide card is 62% of a narrow window and at most 480 dp; the "account" shot of the scratch test.

**NOT run:** API 26; `AccessibilityUiTest` (ATF checks on Search with the new controls), `HomeListTest`/`Home*` classes, `PerformanceBudgetTest`, `RefreshBudgetTest`; the host suites, the native release guard, `tools/ios_build.sh unit`; the iOS compile of this code (all of it is common Compose code; the APIs used are in common); TalkBack; a real phone; the Fold; an iPhone.
- **Timing A/B: NOT measured** (the disk filled during the work and the emulator was shared). The marks and the script are in place: build `:androidApp:assembleBenchmark` of commit `02f1a4e` (the parent of the redesign, with the marks only) and of the redesign, seed once (`pm clear`, `--ei seed_rows 100000 --ez seed_full true`), then `python3 tools/search_ab.py base.apk final.apk 3 8` (cold start, tap Search, type "Merchant 17"; tab-to-composed and last-input-to-results medians, alternating rounds, host load printed). Expected from the code: no extra query (the same `search()` call), one list less on the page; the first composition has a Row and a chip row more than before and no filters block. **This is an expectation, not a measurement.**

**Decisions.** One chip row instead of two (a "quick filters" row and an "active filters" row would say the same thing twice); live changes with Apply = close (a draft state would need a second search for the count); Reset and Clear all are two different things on purpose; saved searches on the start page as well as in the panel (the start page is where a person looks first), with different tags; a panel on the page, not a window or a Material bottom sheet (keyboard and touch problems with windows are in this repo's history); the summary hides when nothing matches; the Material `FilterChip` for every chip with an explicit on-colour.

**For the integration run (device, API 34 and API 26, batches of at most 6):** `SearchFiltersUiTest`, `SearchUiTest`, `SearchDatesUiTest`, `SavedSearchUiTest`, `SearchStartUiTest`, `SearchBulkCategoryUiTest`, `AccountPeriodUiTest`, `TagsUiTest`, `BulkEditTest`, `ChangeTypeUiTest`, `WidePaneUiTest`, `UxPolishUiTest`, `AccessibilityUiTest`, `RecreateStressTest`, `KeyboardShortcutsUiTest`, `MotionUiTest`, `HomeFilterScrollTest`, every `Home*` class, `PerformanceBudgetTest`, `RefreshBudgetTest`; then the timing A/B above and the six screenshot sets again.

## Release 0.12.0 integration (Android code 41, iPhone build 38): verified on the final commit

Content since 56696a3 (0.11.0 was built but never uploaded; production is 0.10.6, code 39): Slices 103 to 109. **Two integrators ran at once by mistake** (a second session stacked `6c80088` and `1ec3c33` on this one's commits, and a detached script of it took the emulator lock and killed two device runs and the first timing gate); the other session's scripts were stopped, its two commits were reviewed (below) and kept, and everything in this section was re-run on the final commit by one integrator. Final head: see `git log` (the last commit is this note; product code is that of `366897a`).

**What this integrator (WidgetFixer) fixed, in `441d601`, `61be781`, `994a4db`:** amount-band suggestions were never wired into To review (`advise`/`adviseIncome` got no `band`; income history now from `CategoryEvidence`), a shared text re-opened Check a message after every app-lock cycle (consume-once `sharedTextTaken`), a second share while the page was open showed the old text (`key(handledShared)`), a stale Check result could overwrite a newer one (generation counter), the restore dialog's text column now scrolls (cut at font 2.0), the privacy documents mention Check a message. Test fixes (all real test bugs, found by running, not product bugs): `GuidedSetupUiTest` assumed Close/Done/the checkup return to Home (they return to where the setup was opened, More) and that Cash/Bank without digits do not count; `SameShopUiTest` tapped Remove under the join bar (the touch landed on its Undo; the button is now pressed by its action); `CaptureRulesUiTest` looked for a rule in a lazy list that was not scrolled to it (taller screens); `ZzCheckupResumeUiTest` bound the listener with the bare setting (Android 14 needs `cmd notification allow_listener`); `AppLockTest` moved the fake clock before backgrounding; lost-tap repeats in `CaptureRulesUiTest`, `DataTest`, `CategoryKindsUiTest`, `SkippedSendersUiTest`. `tools/release_smoke.py`: after Search the first Home card is looked for after scrolling to the top (a first-run Home is taller than the screen), and the launcher drop is confirmed with a tap at 66% of the height (at 85% the launcher's predicted apps opened Files).

**Code review (five read-only reviewers, each reading the code, not the reports) and what was fixed in `6c80088`:**
- Fix this balance: every balance reader (accountBalances, accountBalanceBefore, in-memory balances, card statement, utilisation, due reminder, safe to spend) starts from the opening balance; Undo was not one-shot (a second call moved the opening balance again): it now needs the fix's history line. The restore rule "a fresh phone takes the opening balance of the default Cash and Bank" needs no live entry, id `cash` or `bank`, the phone's account at zero opening with the same type and currency and no use at all (entries live or deleted, quick entries, repeating items): it cannot overwrite an existing account's balance. New `BalanceFixReviewTest` restores the 0.9.0, 0.10.4 and 0.11.0 payloads into a phone with Cash and Bank (balances equal an empty phone's, second restore changes nothing, an entry blocks the rule).
- Replaced-entry records: a restore takes a live entry away only with a record and something live later in its chain; a record chain is now cut at 64 links (a damaged file chaining 20,000 live entries walked each chain to the end: quadratic). Limit kept and documented: an edit made to an entry that is then taken away is not carried to its replacement.
- Merchant aliases and amount bands: aliases only write settings (never an entry); users with no alias run the plain search; amount-band SURE needs 3 agreeing and none disagreeing; the 441d601 wiring is equal to the old inline income history. Fixed: entries waiting for a choice learned before 0.12 under the name's own key (`amazonpay`, `inox`, ...) were no longer found for "File N earlier like this"; the Categorise review and Check a message ignored the person's aliases; the income suggestion used INR whatever the ledger's currency; chains of aliases deeper than 8 (hand-made backup) are refused; a search of a joined shop looks for at most 6 other names. Known and NOT fixed: the built-in same-merchant table pools learned choices by key, but the history/amount lookup of a name finds the other names' entries only where they share a word (INOX does not see PVR entries); a join can bring a hidden subscription back.
- Check a message: no read of the SMS inbox anywhere (no READ_SMS, no content://sms); the clipboard is read only when Paste is tapped; manifest diff is only the ACTION_SEND text/plain filter (no new permission, INTERNET absent). Fixed: shared words came back when the page was opened again (they are now forgotten when the page is left); a second share while the page was open showed no answer; the share counter was saved and swallowed the first shares after the activity was recreated; a paste is capped at 4,000 characters; "does not look at your messages by itself" was untrue with capture on and now says the check reads only what is pasted or shared; the iPhone Shortcuts row no longer says "the automation is working" when a Paste or a test message also counts; "last payment found" reads the whole log (it said "nothing found yet" after 30 days). `PRIVACY_POLICY.md`, `docs/site/privacy.html` (re-host it) and `PLAY_SMS_DECLARATION.md` say so (also true on iPhone). Not fixed (inferred only): a share made while the app lock is on may open the page blank after unlocking; a share closes a half-typed entry.
- Search and merge sanity: marks log only in debug and benchmark builds; Search results, limit and saved searches unchanged; no orphaned or misplaced member in LedgerViewModel/AppRoot/CaptureScreens; fixed `OPEN_SETUP` (unused) would have stayed hidden behind the checkup, and the "back from Your data" path now clears `importFromEarlier`.
- From the screenshots (360 and 393 dp, light and dark, font 2.0): at 140% text size the To review amount goes under the payee (the payee broke mid-word, "Ama/zon"); the Same shop dialog puts its boxes first (at font 2.0 the explanation hid them).

**What `1ec3c33` added (reviewed, kept):** Undo of a fix shows the card-in-credit hint again (hidden "by a fix" is stored as `fix`; a dismissal by the person stays), the Filters panel remembers what the filters were at opening across a font-size rebuild (`rememberSaveable` with a saver for every `SearchInput` field).

**Verified on the final commit (product code `366897a`):**
- Host JVM `--rerun-tasks`: domain 759, parsers 221, storage 551 = **1,531 tests, 0 failures**. Native release guard (`iosSimulatorArm64ReleaseTest`, `--rerun-tasks`): domain 752, parsers 207, 0 failures. `:shared:compileKotlinIosSimulatorArm64` compiles. `tools/ios_build.sh unit` 41 of 41. `tools/ios_build.sh interop`: the Java reference read all 8 files (two with replaced records).
- Device, API 26 (122 classes, timing classes excluded, cold boot, batches of 6, cold boot every 4 batches): **817 tests**. The first four batches had 14 failures in 10 classes (`AccessibilityUiTest`, `AccountAskUiTest`, `AccountFilterUiTest`, `AccountPeriodUiTest`, `AccountTilesUiTest`, `AmountSignTest`, `AppearanceUiTest`, `BillItemsUiTest`, `BudgetUiTest`, `BulkEditTest`) while an iPhone build and the iOS tests were using the CPU of the Mac; each of the 10 classes (and `SkippedSendersUiTest`) passed alone twice afterwards, and batches 4 to 20 (all the new classes, `SameShopUiTest`, `GuidedSetupUiTest`, `ZzCheckupResumeUiTest`) had no failure. An earlier full run on `6c80088` had one failure (`AppLockTest`, passed 8 times alone afterwards; the ordering fix above is the likely cause, not proven).
- Device, API 34 (72 classes: every new class and every area class of the list below, widgets, `AppLockTest`): **535 tests**, one failure (`SkippedSendersUiTest`, a lost tap on Save; repeat added; 4 of 4 twice on API 34 afterwards). The earlier API 34 failures (`CaptureRulesUiTest`, `CategoryKindsUiTest`, `SameShopUiTest`, `ZzCheckupResumeUiTest`) are the test bugs listed above and pass.
- Release builds: `assembleRelease`, `bundlePlaySms`, `packagePlaySmsUniversalApk`; `tools/check_release_keeps.py` PASS on the APK and on the Play universal APK with the Play mapping; badging 0.12.0 / 41 (Play application id `io.github.chandu007.expenses`, as before); the AAB manifest has RECEIVE_SMS, no INTERNET, and against the 0.11.0 AAB only the `ACTION_SEND` / `DEFAULT` / `text/plain` filter is new. `tools/release_smoke.sh --no-build`: API 26 7 passed + 7 skipped (widgets need Android 12+) twice; API 34 **14 of 14** twice. Upgrade over the production 0.10.6 APK (built from `06c540e`, same key): API 26 and API 34, `adb install -r`, 0.10.6 / 39 to 0.12.0 / 41, the entry of 123 is still listed and found by Search, the widget placed before the upgrade still shows the total after the eye tap and its chip adds an entry (also from a cold process), no widget error in the log, notification channels unchanged.
- Sizes: AAB 35,640,068 bytes (0.10.6: 35,182,606; 0.11.0: 35,314,793; +1.3% over production), release APK 57,179,741, Play universal APK 57,339,320.
- Timing gate A/B against 56696a3 (`PerformanceBudgetTest` + `RefreshBudgetTest`, API 26, cold boot, alternating old/new/new/old/old/new, two rounds of 3 runs each per build, 1-minute host load 3 to 7): nothing consistently slower by 30%. Round one had "save to new row" 17.3/20.8/22.7 ms old against 30.7/20.8/29.1 new and "rebuild after the row is shown" 74 to 90 old against 90 to 115 new (host load 6 to 7); round two (load 3 to 5) has 21.4/22.0/25.8 old against 22.2/20.2/18.6 new and 85/90/92 old against 89/78/70 new. All medians of both rounds together are within 0.62 to 1.24 of old. **The absolute numbers of the old build and the new one both exceed the phone budgets on this emulator today** (for example balances 121 ms against 37.5 ms; search without a match 700 ms against 200 ms), so the budget tests fail for both builds equally; they passed in the 0.11.0 integration. That is the state of this emulator and Mac, not of the change.
- Search A/B (100,000 entries, benchmark build, typed "Merchant 17", baseline `16d5ae8`, 3 alternating rounds of 8): typed-to-results median 642 ms (range 487 to 799, n 24) for the baseline, 604 ms (437 to 997, n 24) for HEAD. `tools/search_ab.py` itself returned no data on this emulator (the downgrade install, the tab position at y 2175 and `pidof`), so a copy with `-d` and a tap at y 2100 was used, and the tab-to-composed mark could not be read because "search: tapped" did not appear in either build: not measured.
- Cold start (`am start -W` TotalTime, 100k dataset, benchmark builds, 3 alternating rounds of 8): median 545 ms baseline (16d5ae8) against 507 ms HEAD.
- Screenshots (API 26 emulator, 393 and 360 dp, light and dark, font 2.0, an 864 dp window), all read: Fix this balance dialog and card hint, Search (start, typing, Filters panel, chips and results, selection), checkup, Check a message results, guided setup, earlier spending, To review with "Same shop as…" and the amount reason line, restore dialog with replaced lines. Fixed from them: restore dialog scrolls; To review amount goes under the payee at 140% text (`6c80088`); Same shop dialog boxes first. **Not fixed:** the title "Why isn't capture working?" is cut to two lines at 360 dp with font 2.0 (the shared top bar allows two lines); a search at 864 dp is one wide column (there is no two-pane Search), and tapping a result opens the edit sheet.
